- **fractal-p1-stakeholders**: 递归式系统工程的 P1 干系人与运行背景阶段。当用户写 docs/SE 的 P1 文档、分析 stakeholder/ConOps/运行场景/系统边界，或说"P1 / 干系人 / 运行概念 / stakeholders / ConOps"时使用。描述系统与现实世界的关系，仍不设计系统内部。 ([marcas-G/fractal](https://github.com/marcas-G/fractal/tree/HEAD/skills/fractal-p1-stakeholders))
- **fractal-p2-requirements**: 递归式系统工程的 P2 系统需求阶段。当用户写需求文档、建 REQ 台账、定义验证标准，或说"P2 / 需求 / requirements"时使用。只定义能力与验证标准，不设计实现。 ([marcas-G/fractal](https://github.com/marcas-G/fractal/tree/HEAD/skills/fractal-p2-requirements))
- **fractal-p3-logical**: 递归式系统工程的 P3 逻辑分解阶段。当用户做功能分解、逻辑流、状态机、派生需求分析，或说"P3 / 逻辑分解 / logical decomposition"时使用。回答"逻辑上必须发生什么"，不绑定任何实现元素。 ([marcas-G/fractal](https://github.com/marcas-G/fractal/tree/HEAD/skills/fractal-p3-logical))
- **fractal-p4-architecture**: 递归式系统工程的 P4 架构设计阶段。当用户分配职责、划分子系统、定接口归属、做架构决策（ADR），或说"P4 / 架构 / architecture"时使用。回答"哪些元素承担哪些逻辑责任"。P4 结束后必须进入递归判断（fractal-recursion-check）。 ([marcas-G/fractal](https://github.com/marcas-G/fractal/tree/HEAD/skills/fractal-p4-architecture))
- **fractal-p5-detail**: 递归式系统工程的 P5 详细设计阶段。当用户对叶子元素做详细设计、定义精确接口契约/不变量/异常语义，或说"P5 / 详细设计 / detailed design"时使用。只对递归判断中判定为"叶子"的元素执行。 ([marcas-G/fractal](https://github.com/marcas-G/fractal/tree/HEAD/skills/fractal-p5-detail))
- **fractal-p6-implementation**: 递归式系统工程的 P6 实现阶段。当用户实现/审查代码或其他产物与设计的一致性、记录实现偏差，或说"P6 / 实现 / implementation review"时使用。回答"产物是否忠实实现既定设计"——先有设计后有实现审查。 ([marcas-G/fractal](https://github.com/marcas-G/fractal/tree/HEAD/skills/fractal-p6-implementation))
- **fractal-p7-verification**: 递归式系统工程的 P7 验证阶段。当用户要验证需求被实现、建证据矩阵、跑验收测试、做 V&V，或说"P7 / 验证 / verification / evidence matrix"时使用。回答"有没有客观证据证明规格被正确实现"（Did we build it right）。与 P8（做的是对的东西）严格区分。 ([marcas-G/fractal](https://github.com/marcas-G/fractal/tree/HEAD/skills/fractal-p7-verification))
- **fractal-p8-validation**: 递归式系统工程的 P8 确认阶段。当用户要验证"系统是否真正解决了最初的问题"、做实战/真实环境验收、写最终判定，或说"P8 / 确认 / validation / did we build the right thing"时使用。回答使命是否完成。与 P7（规格符合性）严格区分。 ([marcas-G/fractal](https://github.com/marcas-G/fractal/tree/HEAD/skills/fractal-p8-validation))

Invoke this through the skill tool, never as an OpenCode command. ([onairmarc/dotfiles](https://github.com/onairmarc/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/audits/fragility-audit) · ★3)
- **fragility-common**: Shared library and file contracts for the fragility skills (system-history, bug-trace, refactor-plan, identify-fragile-systems, investigate-bug) and the findings scripts that file plans to the configured tracker (Jira or GitHub issues). Read when changing how those skills exchange data, or before running findings-doctor, findings-match, or findings-upsert. ([BuilderIO/agent-native](https://github.com/BuilderIO/agent-native/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/fragility-common) · ★7.7k)
- **fragility-commons**: Canonical contracts for proven failure-path audits and their planning, review, split, execution, and verification handoffs. ([onairmarc/dotfiles](https://github.com/onairmarc/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/audits/fragility-commons) · ★3)
- **fragility-flagging**: Identify which specific assumption changes cause conclusion divergence. Rates fragility severity and plausibility of alternatives. ([yogsoth-ai/deep-insight](https://github.com/yogsoth-ai/deep-insight/tree/HEAD/skills/fragility-flagging) · ★1)
- **fragiola-ui**: Build every user interface with Fragiola UI (shadcn-style registry on Base UI + Tailwind v4), following its examples and rules. Use before creating or changing any UI component. ([maurodesouza/ai-kanban-app-lab](https://github.com/maurodesouza/ai-kanban-app-lab/tree/HEAD/lab-09/.agents/skills/fragiola-ui) · ★2 · 4 copies)
- **fragment**: Build, publish and share small stateful, multiplayer web apps (fragments) for the person you work with, on {{platform}}, with the `fragment` CLI. Use when they want an app, a page, a tracker, a shared list or a webhook inbox online at a link, want to change or share one, or want to use their apps as tools. ([finitecomputer/fragment](https://github.com/finitecomputer/fragment/tree/HEAD/cli) · ★3)
- **fragment**: Keep project decisions, current state, and next steps in the repo so a fresh coding agent can resume. Use when installing or updating Fragment in an existing repository. ([hematenergi/fragment](https://github.com/hematenergi/fragment/tree/HEAD/plugins/adopt-fragment/skills/fragment) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **fragment**: Orchestriert die Umsetzung eines einzelnen Schritts aus docs/plan.md nach dem Vibe-Coding-Zyklus (Schritt lesen → Fehlerfall-Tests mitdenken → implementieren → volle QS mit Belegen → Selbst-Review gegen CLAUDE.md → Journal). Verwenden, um einen Plan-Schritt sauber und nachvollziehbar abzuschliessen, z.B. "/fragment Schritt 2" oder "/fragment docs/plan.md Schritt 3: User-Modell". ([ManuGlutz/learningpath](https://github.com/ManuGlutz/learningpath/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fragment))
- **Fragment Assembly**: Join two posed fragments into one molecule that keeps them where they sit, or grow a single posed fragment into open pocket space. Use when the task supplies fragment SDFs with coordinates plus a pocket and asks for a single molecule containing them -- "link these fragments", "connect fragment_0 and fragment_1", "grow this fragment", fragment linking, fragment merging, or scaffold assembly from crystallographic fragment hits. ([NVIDIA-BioNeMo/agent-for-lifesciences](https://github.com/NVIDIA-BioNeMo/agent-for-lifesciences/tree/HEAD/backend/skills/fragment-assembly) · ★9)
- **fragment-adduct-annotation**: Use when confirming fluorescent-labeled N-linked glycan peaks from LC-MS/MS data: after MS1 screening produces an output Excel file and candidate glycan-RT pairs need MS2 fragment-level annotation to verify identity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-adduct-annotation))
- **fragment-analysis**: Analyzes cfDNA fragment size distributions and fragmentomics features using FinaleToolkit or Griffin. Extracts nucleosome positioning patterns, fragment ratios, and DELFI-style fragmentation profiles for cancer detection. Use when leveraging fragment patterns for tumor detection or tissue-of-origin analysis. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/liquid-biopsy-fragment-analysis) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **fragment-analysis**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Oncology/Liquid_Biopsy/fragment-analysis) · ★36 · 4 copies)
- **fragment-assembly-transformer-architecture**: Use when when you have 1D NMR spectra (1H and/or 13C) of an unknown compound with up to ~19 heavy atoms and need to predict both molecular formula and connectivity without manual structure hypothesis generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/fragment-assembly-transformer-architecture) · 2 copies)
- **fragment-authoring**: Author or edit UnicornHack map fragments — the glyph-grid templates that level generation stitches into rooms. Use when: creating a new `NormalMapFragment` / `ConnectingMapFragment` / `DefiningMapFragment`, editing an existing fragment under `src/UnicornHack.Core/Data/Fragments/`, adding edge conditions or annotations, choosing transform flags, writing `GenerationWeight` DSL expressions, picking feature/connection glyphs, or wiring per-tile creature wildcards (`%`) and feature wildcards (`$`). ([AndriySvyryd/UnicornHack](https://github.com/AndriySvyryd/UnicornHack/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/fragment-authoring) · ★60)
- **fragment-based-count-matrix-generation**: Use when you have a backed AnnData object containing processed fragment data (stored in .obsm['fragment_paired'] or . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/fragment-based-count-matrix-generation))
- **fragment-bias-correction**: Use when estimating transcript expression from RNA-Seq alignments (SAM/BAM) whose library preparation (NSR priming, WT SOLiD, strand-specific protocols, single- or paired-end) induces non-uniform fragment distribution and quantification bias. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fragment-bias-correction))
- **fragment-canonicalization-and-matching**: Use when you have a collection of molecular fragments (e.g., from molecular decomposition, retrosynthesis, or synthetic planning) that must be matched to known fragment libraries or standardized representations before feeding them into a transformer assembly model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-canonicalization-and-matching) · 2 copies)
- **fragment-counting**: Fragment-level counting: R1/R2 quality-weighted consensus, DPF/RDF/ADF (FAD/FAF in VCF), QNAME/UMI molecule hashing, duplex/simplex merging, amplicon split. Use when touching shared/fragment.rs or any DPF/RDF/ADF logic, or debugging fragment-level VAF. ([msk-access/gbcms](https://github.com/msk-access/gbcms/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/fragment-counting) · ★2)
- **fragment-end-linear-down-weighting**: Use when reconstructing coverage profiles from directionally extended short ChIP-Seq reads and fragment sizes are only known within a size-selection range (e.g. 175-225 bp), so HDF ends are uncertain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fragment-end-linear-down-weighting))
- **fragment-exact-mass-calculation**: Use when a list of PFAS SMILES structures must be converted into a predicted fragment library (exact masses, fluorine flags, substructure mapping) for fragment-based non-targeted screening in FluoroMatch 2.0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-exact-mass-calculation))
- **fragment-extraction-from-aligned-reads**: Use when sorted BAMs aligned with BWA-MEM (short reads) or minimap2 (long reads) and a bgzipped, indexed variant VCF are ready, and platform-specific fragment files must be produced as input to haplotype phasing tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fragment-extraction-from-aligned-reads))
- **fragment-file-parsing**: Use when you have a fragment file (from 10X/Hi-C linked reads, paired-end NGS, PacBio SMRT or ONT data) plus a VCF of heterozygous variants and need to build SpecHap's linkage graph for spectral-based haplotype assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fragment-file-parsing))
- **fragment-file-validation**: Use when after invoking pp.make_fragment_file to convert a coordinate-sorted BAM file (e.g., from 10X ATAC or standard alignment) into a compressed fragment file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/fragment-file-validation))
- **fragment-formula-annotation-buddy-prediction**: Use when building or extending an MS2 reference spectral library for compound identification and source spectra lack confirmed fragment formula assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-formula-annotation-buddy-prediction))
- **fragment-frequency-filtering**: Use when consolidating multiple deconvolved MS2 spectra (DDA or SWATH-DIA) that belong to the same MS1 peak across replicates, and noisy or inconsistently detected fragments must be removed before building a consensus spectrum. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-frequency-filtering))
- **fragment-frequency-threshold-optimization**: Use when you have replicate MS/MS spectra with labeled fragment recurrence frequencies and need to select an optimal frequency threshold (beyond the default 0.1) that maximizes spectral quality metrics while minimizing false positive noise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-frequency-threshold-optimization) · 2 copies)
- **fragment-geometry-string-parsing**: Use when single-cell FASTQ reads come from protocols whose barcode/UMI layout is not simple fixed-position (e.g. repeated barcode segments like b[16]...b[4], or 10x-style 1{b[16]u[12]x:}2{r:} geometry strings) and must be normalized before mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fragment-geometry-string-parsing))
- **fragment-hunter**: Vicious fragment hunter for fiction prose. Mechanically extracts all 1-3 word sentences from narration using a Python/regex pass, then dispatches focused workers when supported to evaluate context, kill fragments, and rebuild them into cinematically muscular sentences a voice actor can perform. Tracks points and levels. Use when user says "fragment-hunter with a chapter reference" or asks to kill fragments, fix choppy prose, or prep for audiobook narration. ([ArielNya/open-storycraft-app](https://github.com/ArielNya/open-storycraft-app/tree/HEAD/open-storycraft/fragment-hunter))
- **fragment-intensity-aggregation**: Use when after extracting raw MS/MS spectra from mzML files but before consensus spectrum generation, when you observe high-resolution fragment lists where nearby peaks (within a specified mass tolerance, typically 0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-intensity-aggregation) · 2 copies)
- **fragment-intensity-binning-sqrt-transform**: Use when reconstructing GLEAMS' per-spectrum input encoding: fragment peaks from an mzML/peak file must be converted to fixed-length binned intensity features (2449 bins, 50.5–2500 m/z, square-root transformed) before feeding the neural network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-intensity-binning-sqrt-transform))
- **fragment-intensity-heatmap-construction**: Use when after selecting a feature or feature group of interest (e.g. from the t-SNE overview or k-medoid clustering in the specXplore dashboard), you need a single-figure overview of how fragment ions and neutral losses overlap across the multiple MS/MS spectra of that group. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-intensity-heatmap-construction))
- **fragment-intensity-nonlinear-regression-fitting**: Use when building targeted lipidomics PRM/MRM assays that need optimal collision energies per fragment from CE-dependent intensity measurements on Thermo QExactive HF or Agilent QTOF instruments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-intensity-nonlinear-regression-fitting))
- **fragment-intensity-threshold-binning**: Use when you have a feature-lipid candidate table from exact-mass MS1 matching and need to decide which experimental fragments in ddMS2-topN .ms2 data are trustworthy evidence for confirming each lipid. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-intensity-threshold-binning))
- **fragment-intensity-threshold-filtering**: Use when in silico MS/MS fragment predictions have been generated (e.g., by CFM-ID) at multiple energy levels and need to be reduced to the most informative ions before publication or spectral-library search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-intensity-threshold-filtering))
- **fragment-ion-cross-collision-energy-alignment**: Use when MS/MS spectra for the same compound were acquired at multiple collision energies and you need to link identical fragment ions across CEs to construct per-fragment longitudinal intensity vectors before similarity scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-ion-cross-collision-energy-alignment))
- **fragment-ion-database-matching**: Use when you have centroid-mode LC-MS AIF chromatograms processed through xcms and RAMClustR, a feature table with target m/z and retention time values, and access to fragment libraries (e.g., LipidPos for lipids). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-database-matching) · 3 copies)
- **fragment-ion-difference-counting**: Use when when preparing tandem MS/MS data for spectral alignment and similarity comparison, particularly when you have loaded raw fragmentation spectra and need to extract and quantify mass difference patterns that capture the fragmentation process. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-difference-counting) · 3 copies)
- **fragment-ion-intensity-scoring**: Use when iTRAQ/TMT/TMTpro-labeled PSMs need interference QC scores computed per scan from b/y fragment ion intensity fractions in mzML data, to filter compressed reporter ion quantifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/fragment-ion-intensity-scoring))
- **fragment-ion-intensity-vector-comparison**: Use when candidate PSMs must be distinguished from near-identical sequence variants: e.g., scoring the stability of the spectral angle under adjacent-residue swaps using Prosit-predicted fragment-ion intensities in immunopeptidomics rescoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-ion-intensity-vector-comparison))
- **fragment-ion-library-matching**: Use when you have peak-picked experimental MS/MS data (m/z, retention time, intensity) from UHPLC-HRMS/MS instruments (Orbitrap or Q-TOF from Agilent, Bruker, SCIEX, or similar vendors) and need to annotate detected features with specific lipid identifications using in-silico fragmentation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-library-matching) · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-mass-calibration**: Use when when comparing experimental spectra to reference library spectra and fragment ion m/z values show systematic drift or measurement noise that could distort neutral loss peaks or cosine similarity scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-mass-calibration) · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-mass-matching**: Use when you have a tandem mass spectrum (MSMS) of a known or hypothesized peptide, along with its ProForma 2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-mass-matching) · 3 copies)
- **fragment-ion-mass-matching-with-tolerance**: Use when denoising MS/MS spectra and you have: (1) a precursor ion with measured m/z and known molecular formula (from SMILES or direct formula input), (2) an adduct type ([M+H]+, [M+Na]+, etc.), (3) a list of fragment ions with observed m/z values, and (4) a need to distinguish chemically valid. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-mass-matching-with-tolerance) · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-matching**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/agent/fragment-ion-matching) · ★745)
- **fragment-ion-matching**: Use when you have two tandem mass spectra (query and reference) with precursor m/z and fragment ion peaks, and you need to identify which fragment ions correspond between them to assess spectral similarity, detect structural variants, or validate compound identifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-matching) · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-mz-intensity-extraction**: Use when you have an X!Tandem XML output file and need the underlying fragment-ion m/z and intensity values for each parsed spectrum, e.g. for downstream spectral inspection or identification detail tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/fragment-ion-mz-intensity-extraction))
- **fragment-ion-peak-annotation**: Use when you have experimental MS/MS spectra matched against a reference library (via cosine similarity or dot-product scoring) and need to map individual fragment peaks in the experimental spectrum to their corresponding m/z and intensity values in the matched library entry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-peak-annotation) · 3 copies)
- **fragment-ion-peak-annotation**: Use when you have validated peptide-spectrum matches (with known peptide sequences) plus de novo tool predictions and need to quantify missing fragment ions, noise peak fractions, or how these spectrum properties drive peptide recall. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-ion-peak-annotation))
- **fragment-ion-peak-detection-and-normalization**: Use when immediately after loading raw MS/MS spectra from .mgf, .msp, or .mzML files, before generating the bag-of-fragments corpus or extracting neutral losses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-peak-detection-and-normalization) · 3 copies)
- **fragment-ion-scoring-and-ranking**: Use when when you have an experimental MS/MS spectrum (centroid mode) and need to convert it into a scored fragment library entry, or when you must rank candidate fragments by confidence before performing spectrum-to-spectrum matching in metabolite annotation workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-scoring-and-ranking) · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-search-parameter-configuration**: Use when dereplicating experimental CID/HCD tandem MS spectra of NRPs against a CycloBranch sequence database and you must set error tolerances, intensity thresholds, and ion-type filters for a fair tool comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-ion-search-parameter-configuration))
- **fragment-ion-similarity-matrix-construction**: Use when you have tandem-MS peak m/z lists (e.g. from MassBank or NIST Tandem Mass Spectral Library) plus precursor m/z values for one or more spectra and need a quantitative similarity measure between every pair of fragment ions before running spectral alignment or significance testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-ion-similarity-matrix-construction))
- **fragment-ion-similarity-scoring**: Use when comparing two fragmentation spectra whose fragment m/z values do not match exactly and whose precursor ion neutral losses differ (e.g., structurally related compounds with multiple modifications), where cosine scoring fails. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-ion-similarity-scoring))
- **fragment-ion-spectral-matching**: Use when after pointwise correlation analysis and exact mass difference refinement have identified candidate ion-species pairs that may share a common analyte origin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-spectral-matching) · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-theoretical-mass-calculation**: Use when when building a comprehensive lipid fragment ion library covering all chain composition and positional isomer variants (e.g., 168.6 million entries). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-theoretical-mass-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-type-interpretation**: Use when you have an tandem MS spectrum with unidentified peaks and a known or hypothesized peptide sequence (in ProForma 2.0 format, including post-translational modifications). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-type-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-type-matching**: Use when evaluating de novo peptide sequencing inputs on antibody MGF spectra: you need per-cleavage-site ion coverage, missing-fragment statistics, or peptide/noise peak labels for spectral quality assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-ion-type-matching))
- **fragment-length-estimation-cross-correlation**: Use when when beginning ChIP-Seq analysis with single-end BED/SAM input and no prior knowledge of the library's fragment length. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/fragment-length-estimation-cross-correlation))
- **fragment-length-estimation-from-read-pairs**: Use when you have paired-end ChIP-Seq data (BEDPE format) and need to determine the empirical fragment length (insertion length) before peak calling. This is mandatory for paired-end ChIP-Seq workflows to ensure MACS3 correctly extends coverage tracks and calculates local bias; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/fragment-length-estimation-from-read-pairs))
- **fragment-length-prediction-and-extension**: Use when after filtering duplicate reads from ChIP-Seq data but before generating pileup coverage tracks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/fragment-length-prediction-and-extension))
- **fragment-level-spectral-annotation**: Use when MS2 spectra from DDA or SWATH-DIA LC-MS runs (often pooled QC samples) have been deconvoluted into consensus spectra and need fragment-level compound assignments against a reference spectral library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-level-spectral-annotation))
- **fragment-level-spectrum-prediction**: Use when when you have a molecular structure (SMILES, InChI, or chemical formula) and need to predict its tandem mass spectrum for structural elucidation or mass spectrometry validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-level-spectrum-prediction) · 2 copies)
- **fragment-mass-defect-binning**: Use when a model outputs probabilities over chemical subformulae or atom subsets (fragment distribution with exact atomic-composition masses) and these must be decoded into a probability-weighted mass spectrum at a chosen mass resolution (e.g. 1 Da or 0.1 Da). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fragment-mass-defect-binning))
- **fragment-mass-table-generation**: Use when a glycan of interest is absent from the supplied GlyKAn AZ MS1/MS2 Fragment Database and theoretical fragment masses/patterns must be generated to expand the database before MS2 peak confirmation.
